Result: Seq2Seq模型在跨年份泛化上表现良好，R²超过0.8，持续优于传统方法；单峰形状正则化器在几乎不损失预测精度的前提下显著提升了轨迹的合理性。

Conclusion: 研究证明遥感与气象驱动的序列建模能够在景观尺度上学习作物生长动态，为国家级农业监测和产量预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Forecasting crop growth across agricultural landscapes is important for improving the productivity, resilience, and operational management of farming systems. In this work, we investigate whether Earth observation time series and meteorological drivers can be used to predict future canopy development at country scale. We focus on winter wheat and formulate crop growth prediction as forecasting future leaf area index (LAI) trajectories beyond the last available Sentinel-2 observation. We evaluate this task on a multi-year dataset which spans the entire country of Switzerland, containing over 20 million pixel-level Sentinel-2-derived LAI time series paired with meteorological variables. Because cloud cover and revisit gaps leave LAI supervision sparse, models fit the few valid (cloud-free) LAI observations yet oscillate implausibly between them, producing trajectories no real canopy could follow. We introduce a lightweight unimodal shape regulariser which improves trajectory plausibility with negligible loss in accuracy. We compare deep learning sequence-to-sequence (Seq2Seq) models with classic machine learning baselines and show that Seq2Seq models generalise well across years, achieving $\mathrm{R}^2$ above 0.8 and consistently outperforming conventional approaches. Together, these results demonstrate that remote sensing and weather-driven sequence modelling can learn crop growth dynamics at landscape scale. S

</details>


### [51] [MAGneT-3D: Monocular and Domain-Generalizable Temporal 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14282)
*Mohamed Kotb,Johannes Meier,Christoph Reich,Oussema Dhaouadi,Luis Denninger,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAGneT-3D是首个面向域泛化的单目时序3D目标检测方法，通过DRAG自适应生成3D提案和TRIM时序细化与身份合并策略，在零样本跨域场景下（nuScenes、Waymo、Lyft、ONCE）显著提升NDS从12.1%到18.6%，同时保持域内精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于查询的3D检测器依赖可学习的静态查询，其空间分布与训练数据（如视场角）高度耦合。在单目视频场景下，这种过拟合问题尤为严重，导致模型在面对未见数据集和环境时泛化能力差。

Method: 提出MAGneT-3D框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) Domain-Robust Anchor Generator (DRAG)，在推理过程中自适应推导3D提案，替代静态可学习查询；(2) Temporal Refinement and Identity Merging (TRIM)策略，通过时序细化减少对特定3D提案的依赖。此外建立了涵盖nuScenes、Waymo、Lyft和ONCE四个数据集的跨数据集基准。

Result: 在零样本域偏移条件下，MAGneT-3D在所有基线上均取得最优性能，将NDS从12.1%提升至18.6%，同时域内精度也有提升。

Conclusion: 通过消除对静态可学习查询的依赖，并引入时序信息进行提案细化与身份合并，MAGneT-3D有效解决了单目时序3D检测中的域泛化问题，是首个专门针对该任务的域泛化方法。

Abstract: Monocular temporal 3D detection aims to detect objects in 3D, given a monocular video. Query-based 3D detectors unify detection and cross-view association, but their learnable queries fit the spatial distribution of the training data (e.g., field-of-view). We show that this issue is especially severe when these models are applied to monocular video, hindering generalization to unseen datasets and environments. To address this limitation, we introduce MAGneT-3D, the first method for domain-generalized monocular temporal 3D object detection. Instead of relying on static learnable queries, we propose a Domain-Robust Anchor Generator (DRAG) approach that adaptively derives 3D proposals during inference. To further enable domain generalization, we propose a Temporal Refinement and Identity Merging (TRIM) strategy, reducing dependence on specific 3D proposals. To enable comprehensive domain-generalization evaluation, we establish a cross-dataset benchmark spanning nuScenes, Waymo, Lyft, and ONCE. Under zero-shot domain shifts, MAGneT-3D outperforms all baselines, improving NDS from 12.1% to 18.6% while also increasing in-domain accuracy.

</details>


### [52] [Seeing Red, Thinking Bad: Color Bias in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14286)
*Kohsuke Ide,Ryousuke Yamada,Yoshihiro Fukuhara,Hirokatsu Kataoka,Yutaka Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉语言模型如何解读以图像形式呈现的文本，发现仅通过改变文字的视觉样式（如颜色和对比度）即可系统性影响VLM的分析结果，例如将正面词染成绿色会使情感预测偏向正面，降低文本-背景对比度会增加VQA错误，表明VLM对视觉样式存在语义解读偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs正被广泛应用于工业决策系统（如招聘、推荐），因此需要理解VLM如何处理视觉与文本信息。已有研究尚未系统考察渲染文本的视觉样式如何影响VLM的语义分析，这种潜在偏差可能在实际应用中导致错误决策。

Method: 提出"隐形视觉提示"（Stealth Visual Prompts），在不改变文本语义内容的前提下精细改变文字的视觉样式（如颜色、对比度）。通过系统控制文本中单词的视觉样式，测量其对VLM输出的影响，并分析视觉编码器隐层表征的变化来揭示偏差机制。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 将正面词染成绿色会使VLM的情感预测持续偏向正面方向，使其常忽略文本中的负面词；(2) 这种偏差与颜色变化引起的视觉编码器隐层表征改变相关；(3) 降低文本-背景对比度会增加对视觉显著线索的依赖，导致更多错误的VQA输出。

Conclusion: 渲染文本的视觉样式可以引导VLM的解读方向，使其偏离人类的语义理解。这一发现对于确保VLM在工业应用中可靠、公平地处理视觉化文本具有重要意义。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in industrial decision-making systems, such as recruitment support and recommendation. This motivates careful analysis of how VLMs process visual and textual information. In this work, we study how VLMs interpret text rendered as an image, and investigate the influence of visual styling biases. To this end, we introduce Stealth Visual Prompts, which subtly change visual styling of text, such as color and contrast, while preserving semantic content. Using these prompts, we systematically control the visual styling of words in text and measure their impact on the analysis performed by VLMs. We further analyze how such visual perturbations affect the latent representations of the vision encoder. From our experiments, we observed that coloring positive words in green consistently shifts sentiment predictions toward a positive direction. As a result, VLMs often fail to properly account for negative words present in the text. Our analysis suggests that this behavior is correlated with changes in the latent representations of the vision encoder induced by color variations. In addition, we show that reducing text--background contrast increases reliance on visually salient cues and leads to more incorrect Visual Question Answering (VQA) outputs. These results suggest that the visual styling of rendered text can guide VLMs' interpretation in ways that diverge from human semantic understanding.
  Project page: https://github.com/KohsukeIde/color-bias-vlm

</details>


### [53] [Conditional Neural Optimal Transport for Predicting Cellular Phenotypes from Molecular Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14293)
*Gauthier Avité,Maxime Sanchez-Renauld,Nicolas Bourriez,Auguste Genovesio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分子条件化的神经最优传输（Neural Optimal Transport, NOT）模型，用于从分子结构预测细胞表型图像，通过Monge-Gap正则化训练目标，将未处理的阴性对照表型转运至扰动后的表型空间，在未见活性分子上超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高内涵显微镜能够系统性地表征细胞对化学扰动的响应，但化学空间规模巨大，穷举式地表型表征在实验上不可行，因此需要计算模型来预测未经实验处理的分子所诱导的表型，而无需实际获取对应的处理细胞。

Method: 将分子诱导的表型预测建模为图像表示空间中的归纳条件最优传输问题。首先评估经典最优传输基线（静态耦合方法）并发现其在大规模表型图像数据集上效果不佳；随后引入分子条件化的NOT模型，以分子结构作为条件信息，通过Monge-Gap正则化训练目标学习将阴性对照的未扰动表型转运至扰动表型，并采用压缩表示空间进行传输以提升性能和可扩展性。

Result: NOT模型能够恢复分子特异的表型效应，同时减少显微镜相关的技术变异，便于跨实验批次比较。在训练中未见过的活性分子上，模型优于基线方法，证明化学条件化的传输能够泛化到训练未覆盖的分子；进一步分析发现分子编码器是泛化性能的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: NOT为从分子结构和阴性对照表型预测细胞表型提供了有前景的框架，同时指出开发更具信息量的分子表示是提升分布外性能的关键方向。

Abstract: High-content microscopy enables systematic profiling of cellular responses to chemical perturbations, but the scale of the chemical space makes exhaustive phenotypic characterization experimentally infeasible. This motivates computational models that can predict image-derived phenotypes without acquiring the corresponding treated cells. We formulate molecule-induced phenotype prediction as an inductive conditional transport problem in image representation space. Given a negative-control phenotype and the structure of a molecule, we aim to predict the phenotype induced by the corresponding molecule. We first evaluate classical optimal transport baselines and show that static couplings do not yield useful predictions on large-scale phenotypic image datasets. We then introduce a molecule-conditioned Neural Optimal Transport (NOT) model with a Monge-Gap regularization training objective that learns to transport negative-control unperturbed phenotypes toward perturbed phenotypes using molecular structure as conditioning information. NOT recovers molecule-specific phenotypic effects while reducing microscopy-associated technical variation, thereby facilitating comparisons across experimental batches. On unseen active molecules, the model outperforms baseline approaches, demonstrating that chemically conditioned transport can generalize beyond the molecules observed during training. We identified the molecular encoder as the main limitation to this generalization, while transport in a compressed representation space improves performance and scalability. These results establish NOT as a promising framework for predicting cellular phenotypes from molecular structure and negative-control phenotypes, while highlighting the development of more informative molecular representations as a key direction for improving out-of-distribution performance.

</details>


### [54] [Spatial Message Passing in Language Space for Pathology Image Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14309)
*Jing-Cheng Yang,Hao-Jung Wang,Jinhao Du,Yang Hu,Ming-shan Tsai,Jens Rittscher,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SLMP框架，在纯语言空间中对WSI进行空间推理，将病理图像块表示为空间文本图，通过LLM消息传递在相邻图块间聚合信息，无需微调MLLM权重即可提升肿瘤描述准确性并实现可解释的语义优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM能够从组织学图像生成病理描述，但其视觉上下文限制无法直接处理十亿像素级别的WSI。标准的分块（tiling）方法虽然使WSI可处理，却切断了定义肿瘤-间质界面和形态学的组织邻域关系，导致空间上下文丢失。

Method: SLMP将WSI区域表示为空间文本图：节点为MLLM生成的图块描述，边编码空间邻接关系。对每个图块，LLM在共享聚合策略下整合相邻图块的文本消息来细化描述；该策略作为可检查的提示（prompt），可通过文本梯度从模型观察到的组织表型自动优化，无需微调MLLM权重。

Result: 在HER2和CAMELYON16数据集上，SLMP将图块级肿瘤描述准确率提升+3.3至+19.6个百分点，适用于通用和病理专用骨干模型。随机邻居消融实验确认提升源于空间上下文而非额外文本。优化后的策略呈现可解释的、组织特异性的决策规则，并显著缩小通用MLLM与病理专用模型的差距。

Conclusion: SLMP提供了一种透明、灵活的机制，在不更新权重或微调骨干MLLM的情况下，将空间推理整合到基于MLLM的病理分析中，为WSI级病理语言理解提供了新的可行方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can generate pathological descriptions from histological images, but gigapixel Whole Slide Images (WSIs) exceed their visual context limits. The standard tiling workaround makes WSIs tractable yet severs the tissue neighborhoods that define tumor-stroma interfaces and morphology. We introduce Spatial Language Message Passing (SLMP), a framework that performs spatial reasoning entirely in language space, human-readable by construction. SLMP represents a WSI region as a spatial text graph: tiles are nodes initialized with MLLM descriptions, and edges encode spatial adjacency. For each tile, an LLM refines its description by integrating language messages from adjacent tiles under a shared aggregation policy that, on the tile grid, acts as an adaptive local kernel operating on text rather than learned embeddings. This policy is an inspectable prompt that can be refined from model-observed tissue phenotypes via textual gradients, enabling automatic semantic optimization from local cellular context to broader tissue morphology without fine-tuning MLLM weights. On representative HER2 and CAMELYON16 regions, SLMP improves tile-level tumor description accuracy in settings spanning general-purpose and pathology-specialized backbones, with gains of +3.3 to +19.6 percentage points. Random-neighbor ablations confirm that these gains stem from spatial context rather than additional text alone, and inspecting the optimized policies reveals interpretable, tissue-specific decision rules. Besides, without any weight updates or fine-tuning the backbone MLLM, SLMP substantially improves general-purpose MLLMs and narrows its gap to pathology-specialized counterparts, offering a transparent and flexible mechanism for incorporating spatial reasoning into MLLM-based pathology analysis.

</details>


### [55] [Intelligent Detection of Mechanical, Electrical, and Plumbing (MEP) Metrics Based on 2D Floor Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14317)
*Tarandeep Singh Mandhiratta,ANK Zaman,Abdul-Rahman Mawlood-Yunis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Mask RCNN的神经网络模型，用于从2D平面图中自动检测照明符号、识别灯具类型并提取相关文本。模型在COCO数据集格式上训练，取得了良好的检测精度（bbox_mAP=0.7596，segm_mAP=0.7111），旨在加速建筑设计中的机电（MEP）对象检测，提高设计效率并支持节能建筑设计的功率估算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平面图设计中照明符号的识别和灯具类型判定依赖人工，耗时长且效率低。该研究旨在通过自动检测平面图中的照明元素，实现高效的平面设计和每层所需灯具数量与类型的快速估算，从而支持功率需求评估，提升建筑设计流程的效率。

Method: 以Mask RCNN作为基础网络架构，对2D平面图中的照明符号进行标注，并将标注数据转换为COCO数据格式以训练模型。模型用于检测照明符号、识别灯具类型，并提取与灯具相关联的文本信息。

Result: 模型在检测任务中取得bbox_mAP为0.7596，segm_mAP为0.7111；在不同IoU阈值下，bbox_mAP_50达到0.9850，segm_mAP_75达到0.9219，显示出较好的检测与分割性能。

Conclusion: 该模型能够有效自动检测平面图中的MEP对象，帮助建筑、施工等行业缩短设计时间并构建高效工作流，是迈向节能建筑设计的工具开发的第一步。

Abstract: This research developed a neural network-based model to extract various information from 2D floor plans. We detect lighting symbols, identify the appropriate type of light, and extract the associated texts with lights. The study aims to enable efficient floor designing and determining the number and type of lights needed per floor, i.e., allow efficient design and estimate the power requirement of the floor plan. The model was developed using Mask RCNN as the base. The images were annotated and converted into a Coco data format for training the model. The model achieved bbox\_mAP and segm\_mAP values of 0.7596 and 0.7111, respectively. It also performed well at different IoU thresholds, i.e., with bbox\_mAP 50 and segm\_mAP 75 values of 0.9850 and 0.9219, respectively. The developed model will help various industries, such as architecture and construction, to improve design time and create efficient workflows by automatically detecting Mechanical, Electrical, and Plumbing (MEP) objects from floor plans, and it is the first step towards building tools that will help energy-efficient building design.

</details>


### [56] [TRIAGE: Risk-Controlled Pseudo-Label Admission for Annotation-Efficient Semi-Supervised Retinal OCT Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14321)
*Md Ashraful Hossen Akash,Shyla Afroge,Abdullah Al Mamun,Md. Kishor Morol,Tze Hui Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIAGE，一个基于风险控制的半监督框架，用于OCT扫描分类。它通过患者级共形风险控制器与不对称成本矩阵，解决了传统伪标签生成方法未考虑错误类型不对称性的问题。在Noor眼科医院数据集上，仅用20%标注数据即达到89.66%准确率和0.8805宏F1，显著优于六种SOTA半监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT是先进的视网膜疾病诊断成像模态，但缺乏自动化，因为专家标注成本高昂。半监督学习（SSL）可利用未标注B-scan解决标注不足问题，但现有伪标签生成技术多基于预测置信度，未考虑不同类型错误之间的不对称性（例如under-grading与over-grading的经济临床代价不同）。

Method: TRIAGE包含三个核心模块：(1) 层次化分类器，支持疾病亚型的部分异常监督；(2) 患者分组的共形风险控制器，采用原始-对偶覆盖控制；(3) 上下文感知的Transformer教师网络用于跨切片验证。整体框架采用不对称成本矩阵的患者级共形风险控制概念。

Result: 在Noor眼科医院数据集（16,822 B-scans、161位患者、554个体积）上，仅用20%标注数据达到89.66%扫描级准确率、0.8805宏F1、0.9641宏AUC和8.34%的低分级率；仅用5%标注数据保持76.88%准确率和0.1656低分级率。相比六种SOTA半监督方法显著更优，消融实验显示每个模块的贡献，低分级率较固定阈值方法改善42.7%。在OCT-C8数据集上，1%标注达到98.00%准确率（3类），10%标注达到95.94%准确率（8类）。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过将共形预测的不对称风险控制引入半监督框架，有效解决了OCT分类中标注数据稀缺和错误代价不对称的问题，在极低标注比例下仍保持高性能，为医学影像半监督学习提供了新的风险可控范式。

Abstract: The advanced retinal disease diagnosing imaging modality, optical coherence tomography (OCT), encounters a lack of automation because of the high expenses for annotations performed by specialists. The use of SSL solves the problem of insufficient annotations using unlabeled B-scans; however, most of the current techniques for generating pseudo-labels are based on prediction confidence without considering the asymmetry between different types of errors. This paper proposes TRIAGE, a risk-controlled semi-supervised framework for OCT scans classification, which uses the concept of a patient-level conformal risk controller with an asymmetric cost matrix. TRIAGE unites three crucial modules: a hierarchical classifier that is capable of working with partially abnormal supervision of the disease subtypes, a patient-grouped conformal risk controller with primal-dual coverage control, and a context-aware Transformer teacher for cross-slice verification. On the dataset from Noor Eye Hospital (16,822 B-scans, 161 patients, and 554 volumes) with a test set of unseen patients, TRIAGE demonstrates 89.66% scan-level accuracy, 0.8805 macro-F1, 0.9641 macro-AUC, and an 8.34% under-grading rate when using only 20% of the labeled data. With only 5% of the labeled data, TRIAGE keeps 76.88% accuracy and a 0.1656 under-grading rate. Compared with the other six state-of-the-art semi-supervised methods, TRIAGE significantly outperforms them with ablation study demonstrating the contribution of each module in the overall framework performance (by 42.7% in terms of under-grading rate comparing to fixed threshold methods). TRIAGE demonstrates 98.00% accuracy for 3-class classification with 1% labeled data and 95.94% accuracy for 8-class classification with 10% labeled data on the OCT-C8 dataset.

</details>


### [57] [Weakly Supervised Polar Low Segmentation in Sentinel-1 SAR Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14366)
*Andrea Federici,Jakob Grahn,Giacomo Boracchi,Filippo Maria Bianchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CREST，一种基于图像级标签的弱监督语义分割框架，用于在无像素级标注的Sentinel-1 SAR图像中分割极地低压（polar low）。CREST通过约束有序区域扩展模块（CORE）和动态自举损失（DB）改进标准对抗擦除（AER），在极地低压、乳腺超声和PASCAL VOC数据集上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极地低压是高速形成的强烈海上气旋，深度学习可在SAR图像中检测它们，但像素级分割仍是开放挑战：缺乏像素级掩码用于训练，且极地低压的范围本质上是主观的、边界模糊，即使是专家也难以一致地标注。

Method: CREST框架基于对抗擦除（AER），迭代挖掘判别性区域、擦除后重新训练分类器以揭示互补线索作为伪标签。为解决标准AER会收集无关背景特征的问题，CREST引入：(i) 约束有序区域扩展（CORE）模块，编码极地低压的空间连通性先验，从高置信度种子约束区域扩展；(ii) 动态自举（DB）损失，将挖掘顺序作为标签可靠性的代理，衰减来自噪声更大、更晚挖掘区域的监督信号。

Result: 在Sentinel-1 SAR数据上，CREST比标准AER更紧密地跟随气旋结构，并返回多类别掩码（而非二值掩码），其类别表示各区域的可信度。在BUS-UCLM乳腺超声和PASCAL VOC人物数据集上（目标满足相同连通性先验但具有密集掩码），CREST在相同设置下均优于等效AER流程。

Conclusion: CREST通过空间连通性约束和动态自举损失有效提升了弱监督分割中伪标签的质量，在缺少像素级标注的条件下实现了对极地低压更准确的语义分割，并在其他满足连通性先验的任务上展示出泛化能力。

Abstract: Polar lows are intense maritime cyclones that form rapidly at high latitudes. Deep learning can detect them in Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) imagery, but pixel-level segmentation remains an open challenge. No pixel-level masks are available for training, and a polar low's extent is inherently subjective, with diffuse boundaries that even experts delineate inconsistently. We propose Constrained Region Erasing with Soft Targets (CREST), a Weakly Supervised Semantic Segmentation (WSSS) framework that generates masks solely from image-level labels. Our approach builds on Adversarial Erasing (AER), which iteratively mines discriminative regions, erases them, and retrains a classifier to reveal complementary cues that become pseudo-labels for segmentation. However, standard AER also collects irrelevant background features, degrading pseudo-label quality. CREST addresses this with (i) a Constrained Ordinal Region Expansion (CORE) module that encodes the spatial-connectedness prior of polar lows, constraining region expansion from a high-confidence seed, and (ii) a Dynamic Bootstrapping (DB) loss that treats the mining order as a proxy for label reliability, attenuating supervision from noisier, later-mined regions. On Sentinel-1 SAR data, CREST follows the cyclone structure more closely than standard AER, and returns a multi-class rather than binary mask whose classes indicate the reliability assigned to each region. We further evaluate on BUS-UCLM breast ultrasound and PASCAL VOC person data, whose targets satisfy the same connectedness prior but come with the dense masks the SAR data lacks. On both datasets, CREST performs better than the equivalent AER pipeline under identical settings.

</details>


### [58] [GBU-Palm: A Multimodal Video Dataset and Benchmark for Palm Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14389)
*Yingjie Ma,Zitong Yu,Wei Jia,Ajay Kumar,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GBU-Palm，一个大规模多模态视频掌纹呈现攻击检测（PAD）数据集与基准，包含105名受试者、210只手掌、21,326个视频片段，覆盖6种采集环境及bona fide、Print、Replay三类呈现，含6,310个同步RGB-NIR样本，并设计了分离掌纹身份与攻击谱系的泄漏控制协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掌纹PAD数据集受限于静态图像、采集条件单一或多模态视频数据不足，难以在不同环境、模态和攻击类型下进行系统性评估，亟需一个统一且具挑战性的跨环境基准。

Method: 构建包含6种环境和3类呈现类型（bona fide、Print、Replay）的大规模多模态视频数据集；设计泄漏控制协议以分离掌纹身份与攻击谱系；在环境匹配与保留环境两种设置下评估四种代表性视频架构；通过TA/TR/FA/FR分解、频谱掩蔽、时间顺序干预和冻结骨干NIR探测等方法进行行为分析。

Result: 实验结果显示：模型性能在环境偏移下存在显著的架构依赖性退化；RGB-NIR融合并不一致地优于仅使用RGB输入；不同架构在错误模式、频谱利用和时序信息利用上表现出不同特征。

Conclusion: GBU-Palm为跨环境条件下鲁棒多模态掌纹PAD方法的开发与评估提供了统一且富有挑战性的基准，揭示了当前方法在环境泛化方面的不足及多模态融合的局限性。

Abstract: Existing palm presentation attack detection (PAD) datasets are often limited by static imagery, restricted acquisition conditions, or insufficient multimodal video data, hindering systematic evaluation across environments, modalities, and attack types. We present GBU-Palm, a large-scale multimodal video dataset and benchmark containing 21,326 videos from 105 subjects and 210 palms across six acquisition environments, including bona fide, Print, and Replay presentations, with 6,310 synchronized RGB-NIR samples. We construct leakage-controlled protocols that separate palm identity and attack lineage and benchmark four representative video architectures under environment-matched and held-out-environment settings. Results reveal substantial architecture-dependent degradation under environmental shift and show that RGB-NIR fusion does not consistently outperform RGB-only input. We further analyze model behavior through true accept (TA), true reject (TR), false accept (FA), and false reject (FR) decomposition, spectral masking, temporal-order intervention, and frozen-backbone NIR probing, revealing distinct failure patterns and evidence utilization across architectures. GBU-Palm provides a unified and challenging benchmark for developing and evaluating robust multimodal palm PAD methods under cross-environment conditions.

</details>


### [59] [Can We Defend Against AI-Generated Video Attacks on Real-World Crisis Events? A Systematic Evaluation of Detectors, Generators and Social Dissemination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14391)
*Shuo Liang,Yixing Ma,Pengfei Zhou,Zhenglin Wan,Xingyan Chen,Zihan Mei,Manting Li,Feihan Chen,Zhiwen Wang,Bin Xu,Haotian Zhang,Jiajun Song,Shiya Su,Run Liu,Zhenghang Ni,Yifa Yu,Jintao Hong,Bolong Feng,Yifei Liu,Zirui Zhang,Jingxuan Zhang,Songlin Zhao,Yifan Bai,Kang Tan,Yizhe Liu,Junhao Du,Yongtao Ge,Zhaopan Xv,Xinyuan Zhang,Mengru Ma,Chunhua Shen,Wei Wang,Yang You,Zheng Zhu,Kaipeng Zhang,Wangbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-Bench，一个以真实视频为锚点的AI生成视频检测基准，包含17,886个视频（1,830个真实视频锚点覆盖10个社会风险类别，16,056个生成片段来自四种开源和五种闭源生成器）。评估发现：三种检测器家族（传统检测器、零样本多模态模型、微调的MLLM）均无法在RA-Bench上一致泛化；生成条件（质量、条件信息、采样种子）对不同检测器家族影响不同；能误导人类的视频同样难以被检测器识别，且社交传播进一步降低检测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器能制造逼真的战争、灾难、公共紧急事件等真实世界危机场景，带来严重的错误信息风险。然而，现有基准缺乏对检测器和生成器在此类场景下行为的系统性证据，包括检测能力如何随生成条件变化、人们如何感知生成视频、以及检测器在社交传播中是否仍然可靠。

Method: 构建RA-Bench基准，采用真实视频作为锚点，包含10个社会风险类别和9个视频生成器（4个开源+5个闭源），并从三个维度进行评估：1) 检测器泛化能力（7种传统检测器、10种零样本多模态模型在三种审查设置下、2种专门微调的MLLM）；2) 检测能力与生成质量、条件信息和采样种子之间的关系；3) 人类真实性判断与社交传播情境下的检测器可靠性。

Result: 三种检测器家族均无法在RA-Bench上一致泛化；生成属性对不同检测器家族有差异化影响，但源码级检测模式在不同种子间保持稳定；能误导人类的视频难以被当前检测器识别，社交传播会使检测更加困难。

Conclusion: 当前检测方法难以检测逼真的AI生成视频，迫切需要开发对演化中的视频生成器具有鲁棒性的检测器。

Abstract: Recent video generators can fabricate realistic depictions of wars, disasters, public emergencies, and other real-world crises, creating substantial risks of misinformation. Existing benchmarks, however, provide limited evidence on detector and generator behavior in such settings, including how detectability varies with generation conditions, how people perceive generated videos, and whether detectors remain reliable during social dissemination. To address this gap, we introduce RA-Bench, a benchmark for AI-generated video detection that uses Real videos as Anchors. RA-Bench contains 17,886 videos, comprising 1,830 real-video anchors across 10 social-risk categories and 16,056 generated clips from four open-source and five closed-source generators. Based on RA-Bench, we organize our evaluation along three dimensions. We first assess detector generalization across seven traditional detectors, ten zero-shot multimodal models under three review settings, and two MLLMs specifically fine-tuned on AI-generated video detection. Across these methods, none of the three detector families generalizes consistently across RA-Bench instances. We then examine how detectability varies with generation quality, conditioning information, and sampling seeds. These analyses show that generation properties affect detector families differently, while source-level detection patterns remain stable across seeds. Finally, we study human authenticity judgments and detector reliability during social dissemination. We find that videos that mislead people are also difficult for current detectors, and that social dissemination makes detection harder. Together, these findings show that current methods struggle to detect realistic AI-generated videos, highlighting the need for detectors robust to evolving video generators.

</details>


### [60] [IRGNN: Efficient Invariant Radar Graph Neural Network for Radar Point Cloud Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14394)
*Xiao Guo,Wanke Xia,Lili Yang,Caicong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IRGNN，一种用于雷达点云目标检测的不变雷达图神经网络。IRGNN将稀疏雷达点云重构为具有平移和旋转不变特性的图表示，通过改进的消息传递神经网络（含残差连接和虚拟节点层）增强局部特征传播与全局上下文建模，最终在RadarScenes数据集上超越现有雷达目标检测方法，同时显著降低推理时的计算成本和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知中，LiDAR方法在恶劣天气下可靠性下降；雷达点云虽对恶劣天气和低光照具有鲁棒性，但其稀疏、无序、信息量少的特点使得现有基于LiDAR的感知方法难以直接适用，需要专门针对雷达点云特性的目标检测方法。

Method: IRGNN首先利用平移和旋转不变的特征设计将雷达点云重构为图表示，以鲁棒地建模稀疏雷达测量；然后采用带有残差连接和虚拟节点层的改进消息传递神经网络（MPNN），以增强局部特征传播和全局上下文建模；最后使用任务特定头对学习到的图表示进行目标分类和边界框预测。

Result: 在RadarScenes数据集上的实验表明，IRGNN优于现有基于雷达的目标检测方法，取得了有竞争力的性能；同时在推理阶段显著降低了计算成本和内存使用。

Conclusion: IRGNN证明了利用图神经网络和不变性特征设计可以有效地处理稀疏雷达点云进行目标检测，在保持高性能的同时实现了高效的推理，展示了其在自动驾驶中实用化的潜力。

Abstract: Perception is a fundamental component of autonomous driving systems. While LiDAR-based methods have achieved remarkable progress in object detection, their reliability can degrade under adverse weather conditions. Radar point clouds provide a robust alternative due to their resilience to bad weather and low-illumination scenarios. However, radar point clouds are typically sparse, unordered, and less informative than LiDAR data, making it challenging to directly apply existing LiDAR-based perception methods. To address these challenges, we propose IRGNN, an Invariant Radar Graph Neural Network for radar point cloud object detection. IRGNN first reconstructs radar point clouds into graph representations using translation- and rotation-invariant feature designs, enabling robust modeling of sparse radar measurements. It then employs an improved message passing neural network (MPNN) with residual connections and a virtual node layer to enhance local feature propagation and global context modeling. Finally, task-specific heads are applied to the learned graph representations for object classification and bounding box prediction. Experimental results on the RadarScenes dataset show that IRGNN outperforms existing radar-based object detection methods and achieves competitive performance. In addition, IRGNN significantly reduces computational cost and memory usage during inference, demonstrating its effectiveness and practical potential for efficient radar-based perception in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [61] [CRAFT: Constrained Reward via Attention Fine-Tuning for Subject Personalization without Composed Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14403)
*Jihun Park,Kyoungmin Lee,Jongmin Gim,Hyeonseo Jo,Jaeyeul Kim,Han Zou,Zhenpeng Zhan,Yan Zhang,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRAFT是一个单步ReFL框架，通过LoRA适配器微调预训练的reference-aware MMDiT，仅使用1万张参考图像和主体掩码（无需composed-target监督），通过注意力级奖励实现"Where to look"原则，在FLUX.2-klein-9B上达到SOTA，超越了需要15万到200万composed-target对的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主体驱动图像个性化方法依赖大规模paired(reference, composed-target)数据，需要多阶段昂贵的数据构建流程（LLM提示生成、T2I合成、主体提取、VLM质量过滤、对应标注），且紧耦合于特定的目标合成器和数据选择策略。作者旨在消除对composed-target监督的依赖，大幅降低数据成本。

Method: CRAFT采用单步ReFL框架，仅需1万张参考图像和主体掩码。其核心是"Where to look"原则：(1) 注意力级奖励对齐噪声token和短语token的注意力到正确的参考主体；(2) 由此得到的逐主体注意力掩码门控像素级身份奖励，确保图像空间监督与学习的注意力路由一致。通过LoRA适配器微调预训练的reference-aware MMDiT（FLUX.2-klein-9B）。

Result: 在XVerseBench上达到SOTA性能，无需任何composed-target监督，仅用1万张reference-only样本，而先前广义方法需要15万到200万以上composed-target对。该配方可迁移至其他reference-aware骨干网络并持续提升性能。

Conclusion: CRAFT证明注意力级奖励与紧凑的reference-only数据构建足以超越依赖大规模composed-target监督的通用方法，显著降低了主体个性化训练的数据成本，同时保持了跨骨干网络的泛化能力。

Abstract: Subject-driven image personalization---generating new images that preserve the identity of one or several reference subjects in novel scenes---is a foundational capability for modern visual content creation. It is currently dominated by generalized methods that fine-tune a pretrained multimodal diffusion transformer (MMDiT) on hundreds of thousands to millions of paired \emph{(reference, composed-target)} examples, where each composed target is a synthesized image of the subject in a novel scene. Producing such targets demands a costly multi-stage curation pipeline---LLM-based prompt generation, T2I-based composed-target synthesis, reference-subject extraction, VLM-based quality filtering, and correspondence labeling---and tightly couples each method to a particular target synthesizer and curation choice. We introduce \emph{CRAFT} (Constrained Reward via Attention Fine-Tuning), a single-step ReFL framework that fine-tunes a pre-trained \emph{reference-aware} MMDiT via LoRA adapters using a compact reference-only data construction---$10$K reference images and subject masks, with no composed-target supervision. CRAFT realizes a \emph{Where to look} principle: attention-level rewards align noise- and phrase-token attention with the correct reference subject, and the resulting per-subject attention masks gate a pixel-level identity reward to keep image-space supervision consistent with the learned attention routing. Applied to FLUX.2-klein-9B, CRAFT achieves state-of-the-art performance on XVerseBench \rev{while using no composed-target supervision---only $10$K reference-only samples, whereas prior generalized methods require $150$K to over $2$M composed-target pairs}. The same recipe transfers to other reference-aware backbones, consistently improving performance. Project page: https://jihun999.github.io/projects/CRAFT/.

</details>


### [62] [GhostPoint: Self-Supervised Representation Learning by Hallucinating Occluded LiDAR Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14428)
*Mohamed Abdelsamad,Bin Yang,Michael Ulrich,Miao Zhang,Yakov Miron,Alexandru Paul Condurache,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GhostPoint，一种针对LiDAR点云的3D目标检测自监督学习框架，通过实例体素膨胀在局部邻域内生成幻觉潜在特征，并引入预测器级监督，解决现有SSL方法仅约束可见表面导致检测性能受限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR自监督学习方法的目标函数仅定义在可见表面的测量返回上，未约束被遮挡和未观测区域，导致这些方法在逐点任务（如语义/全景分割）上表现良好，但迁移到需要鲁棒缺失结构的3D检测时效果较弱。

Method: 1) 提出实例体素膨胀（instance voxel dilation）机制，在检测到的实例局部邻域内生成幻觉特征；2) 编码器处理观测返回，额外的预测器从观测上下文推断邻域表示；3) 除标准编码器级监督外，引入预测器级监督：可见（可见/掩码）体素匹配教师编码器目标，未观测体素匹配教师预测器幻觉，显式建模超出观测范围的结构。

Result: 在nuScenes和Waymo数据集上达到最先进性能，持续提升下游3D检测效果，尤其在稀疏扫描和有限标签场景下提升显著。

Conclusion: 通过对未观测结构的显式建模，GhostPoint有效弥补了现有SSL方法在3D检测任务上的传输差距，验证了预测器级监督和实例体素膨胀策略的有效性。

Abstract: 3D object detection from LiDAR point clouds is a core problem in autonomous driving. Recent advances in self-supervised learning (SSL) enable scalable pretraining and transfers well to per-point tasks such as semantic and panoptic segmentation, but transfer to 3D detection remains weaker. We analyze recent SSL methods and find that most objectives are defined only on measured LiDAR returns from visible surfaces, leaving occluded and unobserved regions unconstrained. This visible-surface bias can be sufficient for point-wise prediction, but 3D detection requires robustness to missing structure. To address this gap, we propose GhostPoint, an SSL framework that hallucinates latent features in local neighborhoods around discovered instances, generated via a novel instance voxel dilation. In GhostPoint, an encoder processes observed returns, and an additional predictor infers neighborhood representations from observed context. In addition to standard encoder-level supervision, we introduce a predictor-level supervision scheme on sampled voxels from generated neighborhoods. Specifically, observed (visible/masked) voxels match teacher-encoder targets, while unobserved voxels match teacher-predictor hallucinations. This design encourages the learned representation to explicitly model structure beyond observed returns. Extensive evaluations on nuScenes and Waymo demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, consistently improving downstream 3D detection, especially under sparse scans and limited labels.

</details>


### [63] [Style or Signature? Artist-Disjoint Evaluation of Style Classification in Frozen Vision Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14435)
*Rory Ashton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出，使用CLIP等冻结图像嵌入模型进行绘画风格分类时，报告的高准确率可能反映的是对个别艺术家的识别而非对风格的理解。作者提出了一种“艺术家分离”评估协议（即测试时不包含同一艺术家的任何其他作品），在320幅画作、四个20世纪艺术运动的平衡数据集上进行重评估，发现5-NN风格准确率从0.87降至0.77，且下降极不均衡：印象派和立体主义几乎不受影响，而超现实主义下降20个百分点。该模式在四个图像编码器中一致存在，包括纯视觉自监督模型，表明问题源于视觉结构而非语言。作者认为艺术家分离式评估是衡量冻结嵌入中风格理解的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准评估使用随机划分数据集，同一艺术家的作品会同时出现在训练和测试集中，这使分类器可以通过识别画家而非理解艺术运动来获得高准确率。为探究CLIP等模型的高风格分类准确率是否真实反映对风格的理解，作者设计了艺术家分离协议以消除这种混淆因素。

Method: 采用留一（艺术家）验证协议，逐一排除每位艺术家的全部作品，确保分类时不会使用同一画家的其他作品。在包含320幅画作、覆盖四个20世纪艺术运动（印象派、立体主义、超现实主义等）的平衡数据集上，使用5-NN进行分类，并对比四种不同的图像编码器（包括视觉仅自监督模型），以检验效果是否源于视觉结构或语言信息。

Result: 艺术家分离协议下，5-NN风格准确率从0.87降至0.77。下降幅度极不均衡：印象派和立体主义轻微下降，而超现实主义骤降20个百分点。该模式在四种图像编码器中均一致出现，包括纯视觉自监督模型，表明问题根植于视觉结构而非语言信号。在能捕捉真正共享形式的编码器中，个别艺术家难被识别但风格分类稳健；而超现实主义则呈现相反情况。

Conclusion: 艺术史风格分类评估必须采用艺术家分离协议，否则报告的高准确率可能只是画家识别能力的副产品，无法真实反映模型对风格的理解。风格分类评估需要区分“认出画家”与“理解风格”两种能力。

Abstract: Frozen image embeddings from models such as CLIP are increasingly used to classify paintings by art-historical style, with high reported accuracy. We ask whether this accuracy reflects an understanding of style or the recognition of individual artists. Standard evaluation uses random splits in which works by the same artist appear on both sides, so a classifier can succeed by recognising the painter rather than the movement. We re-evaluate style classification under an artist-disjoint protocol, holding out every artist in turn so that no work is ever classified using other works by its own painter. On a balanced dataset of 320 paintings across four twentieth-century movements, 5-NN style accuracy falls from 0.87 to 0.77 under this protocol, and the drop is sharply uneven. Impressionism and Cubism barely move, while Surrealism falls twenty points. The pattern holds across four image encoders, including a vision-only self-supervised model, which places the effect in visual structure rather than language. Where an encoder captures genuine shared form, individual artists are barely recognisable yet style is robust, while Surrealism shows the opposite. We argue that artist-disjoint evaluation is necessary to measure stylistic understanding in frozen embeddings.

</details>


### [64] [Marionette: Predicting World States, Rendering Geometry, Painting Appearance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14530)
*Zian Meng,Zhen Li,Chuanhao Li,Qiang Li,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Marionette，一种用于交互式游戏的世界模型。它将世界状态预测与外观合成解耦：先用两阶段自回归动力学模型预测显式的3D世界状态（包括多实体骨骼、轨迹和旋转），再通过零参数图形渲染器计算世界空间几何与遮挡，最后用视频扩散模型合成逼真RGB外观。实验表明该方案状态可控、长期行为可在状态层修复，且外观保真度不损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式游戏世界模型直接在像素或潜空间自回归预测视觉观测，导致姿态、几何、遮挡等结构化属性被隐式地由同一生成序列维护。在长时程中，这些潜空间属性的误差不断累积，使模型的一致性和可控性变得脆弱。

Method: Marionette由三部分组成：(1) 两阶段自回归动力学模型预测276维的显式可解释3D世界状态，包含多实体关节骨架、度量根轨迹和旋转；(2) 零参数图形桥接器将预测状态转为姿态控制视频，以闭式解析计算世界空间的几何与遮挡；(3) 控制条件下的视频扩散观测模型，从结构化控制信号合成逼真的RGB观测。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 预测的世界状态可直接控制——强行注入不匹配的动作流使根对齐关节误差改变31%；(2) 长期行为由状态决定且可在状态层修复——不加约束时角色漂移至21.2米间距（记录的会话约5米），1/3帧出现地面穿透；在显式状态上施加地形碰撞器和分离上限两条规则后，穿透减少66%，角色保持互动，无需修改观测模型。外观保真度方面，FVD为831，对比记录姿态的799。

Conclusion: 该研究表明将精确几何计算委托给固定的零参数渲染器、而让神经模型专注于外观生成，能够在不损失外观逼真度的前提下获得更强的可控性与长期一致性，为构建可交互、可修复的游戏世界模型提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Interactive game world models typically autoregress visual observations directly in pixel or latent space, forcing structured properties such as pose, geometry, and occlusion to be implicitly maintained by the same generative sequence. Over long horizons, errors in these latent world properties accumulate, making consistency and controllability fragile. We explicitly model the evolving world state, delegate exact geometric computation to a fixed, zero-parameter renderer, and leave the neural model to synthesize appearance. We instantiate this idea as Marionette, a world model for interactive games with articulated characters. First, a two-stage autoregressive dynamics model predicts an explicit and interpretable 276-dimensional 3D world state comprising multi-entity articulated skeletons, metric root trajectories, and rotations. Second, a zero-parameter graphics bridge converts the predicted state into pose-control videos, computing world-space geometry and occlusion in closed form. Third, a control-conditioned video-diffusion observation model synthesizes photorealistic RGB observations from the resulting structured controls. Our experiments establish two properties of Marionette. First, the predicted world state is directly controllable. Forcing a mismatched action stream changes root-aligned joint error by 31% across 48 held-out segments. Second, long-horizon behaviour is determined in the state, and can be repaired there. Left free, the two generated characters drift to 21.2 m apart (recorded sessions stay near 5 m) and a third of frames show ground penetration. Two rules imposed on the explicit state, a terrain collider and a separation cap, cut penetration by 66% and keep the pair engaged, with no change to the observation model. Routing appearance through the predicted state costs no fidelity we can detect, at an FVD of 831 against 799 for recorded pose.

</details>


### [65] [Decoding the Past: An Uncertainty-Aware Deep Learning Framework for Sex Attribution in Prehistoric Hand Stencils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14539)
*Karel Becerra,Boris Mederos,Dean Snow,Ramón A. Mollineda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不确定性感知的深度学习框架，用于旧石器时代手印模板的性别鉴定，通过双图像处理、轮廓提取、结构增强、模型集成和三角化验证，将不确定性作为可量化成分纳入考古学推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旧石器时代手印模板的性别鉴定因缺乏真实标签、当代与史前人群差异及图像退化而极具挑战。传统形态测量方法存在性别间结构重叠大、跨种群泛化差和特征工程主观性等问题，亟需一种鲁棒且可解释的自动化方法。

Method: 提出不确定性感知深度学习框架：(1)融合双图像处理和双轮廓提取以捕获边界信息；(2)通过结构化剪影增强为每个手印模板生成十二种可能的轮廓实现；(3)使用两个集成模型（各含十个EfficientNet-B3和MobileViT-S网络），基于14,036个当代手样本训练；(4)采用三角化验证方案，集成预测结合UMAP+k-NN无监督2D流形映射和LayerCAM可解释AI空间归因，确保解剖一致性。

Result: 在当代数据上，集成模型在较大年龄组中分类准确率超过88%。应用于史前手印时，框架同时输出性别预测和内部一致性置信度，能区分形态稳定与模糊案例。集成预测、潜在空间结构和可解释性分析之间的收敛表明不确定性成为考古推断的可测量组成部分。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了不确定性可以作为可量化的指标融入考古学推断流程，使得古代岩画的性别解码既具备鲁棒性又具备可重复性，为史前艺术研究提供了新的方法论工具。

Abstract: Determining the biological sex of the individuals who created Upper Paleolithic hand stencils remains a challenging problem due to the absence of ground truth, population differences between contemporary and prehistoric groups, and the uncertainty introduced by image degradation. Traditional morphometric methods suffer from high structural overlap across sexes, poor cross-population generalizability, and subjective feature engineering. This study presents an uncertainty-aware deep learning framework for sex attribution in prehistoric hand stencils that explicitly models, propagates, and aggregates uncertainty throughout the analytical pipeline. The methodology combines dual image processing, dual contour extraction, structured silhouette augmentation, model architectural diversity, and ensemble-based decision aggregation. The pipeline generates twelve plausible silhouette realizations per stencil to capture boundary uncertainties, which are processed by two ensembles of ten deep neural networks each (EfficientNet-B3 and MobileViT-S) trained on 14,036 contemporary hand samples. Furthermore, a triangulated validation scheme integrates ensemble predictions with unsupervised 2D latent-space manifold mapping (UMAP + k-NN) and explainable AI spatial attributions (LayerCAM) to ensure anatomical consistency. On contemporary data, ensemble models achieve strong classification performance, with accuracies exceeding 88% in older age groups. When applied to prehistoric stencils, the framework produces both sex predictions and confidence measures of internal agreement, enabling the distinction between morphologically stable and ambiguous cases. Convergence across ensemble predictions, latent-space structure, and interpretability analyses shows that uncertainty can become a measurable component of archaeological inference, enabling robust and reproducible decoding of ancient rock art.

</details>


### [66] [MagnifiQ: Patch-aware Text Guided Progressive Upscaling for High-Resolution Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14543)
*Mahesh Reddy,Yashesh Savani,Antoine Mercier,Hong Cai,Fatih Porikli,Guillaume Berger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MagnifiQ是一个基于扩散模型的图像恢复框架，通过渐进式多分辨率上采样策略（如1024x1024到4096x4096），将SDXL等预训练文本到图像扩散模型的可扩展推理适配为线性复杂度（用卷积替换自注意力层），并利用patch级文本提示提供局部语义指导，在4K分辨率下实现更清晰、更连贯的图像恢复结果，同时兼顾推理速度与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率（尤其4K）图像恢复面临两大挑战：一是需要在全局结构一致性与局部细节恢复之间取得平衡；二是直接在4K分辨率上进行基于扩散模型的恢复计算开销巨大，且容易产生重复或不一致的纹理伪影。

Method: 1) 将预训练文本到图像扩散模型（如SDXL）中的原始自注意力层替换为卷积操作，使计算成本随图像分辨率线性增长，实现可扩展的高分辨率推理；2) 提出渐进式上采样策略，在多个分辨率阶段迭代恢复，逐步细化中间输出而非直接幻化最终4K图像，提升全局连贯性并减少高分辨率伪影；3) 使用patch级特定文本提示提供空间定位的语义引导，增强局部细节的同时控制内容漂移。

Result: 在合成和真实退化图像上的大量实验表明，MagnifiQ在感知质量和人类偏好方面优于先前的基于扩散的图像恢复方法，能够产生更清晰的纹理和更连贯的4K结果，同时通过可扩展骨干结构和渐进式设计提供实用的速度-质量权衡。

Conclusion: MagnifiQ通过线性复杂度骨干、渐进式上采样和patch级文本提示三种核心设计的协同，有效解决了高分辨率扩散图像恢复中计算昂贵和纹理不一致的问题，在4K级别实现了优越的感知质量与实用效率的平衡。

Abstract: High-resolution image restoration from degraded inputs is challenging because it must preserve global structural consistency while recovering fine-grained local details, especially at 4K resolution where direct diffusion-based restoration is computationally expensive and prone to repeated or inconsistent textures. In this work, we introduce MagnifiQ, an image restoration framework that progressively upscales and restores images across resolutions, e.g., from 1024x1024 to 4096x4096. Our approach leverages a pre-trained text-to-image diffusion model such as SDXL and adapts it for more scalable high-resolution inference by replacing its original self-attention layers with convolutional operations whose computational cost grows linearly with image resolution. We further propose a progressive upscaling strategy that iteratively restores images over multiple resolution stages, refining each intermediate output rather than directly hallucinating the final 4K image, thereby improving global coherence and reducing high-resolution artifacts. To enhance local details while controlling content drift, MagnifiQ uses patch-specific text prompts that provide spatially localized semantic guidance during restoration. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world degraded images show that MagnifiQ outperforms prior diffusion-based restoration methods in perceptual quality and human preference, producing sharper textures and more coherent 4K results while offering practical speed--quality trade-offs through its scalable backbone and progressive design.

</details>


### [67] [CPI-Bench: A Comprehensive,Practical and Intelligent Benchmark for Real-World Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14546)
*Qinye Zhou,Jun Zheng,Yongchao Du,Yuan Wang,Zhengrui Chen,Zuan Gao,Taihang Hu,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Xingjian Wang,Zhao Wang,Zhengtao Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Zhengze Xu,Hao Yan,Denghui Yang,Yuhang Yu,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CPI-Bench，一个全面、实用且智能的真实世界图像编辑基准，包含通用、实用和智能三个子集，能够有效区分不同模型的性能并高度对齐人类主观偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准局限于简单单图任务，覆盖维度有限，无法有效区分不同模型间的性能差异，也难以评估模型在复杂多图编辑、高要求推理指令和实际部署场景中的表现。

Method: CPI-Bench包含三个核心子集：CPI-General-Bench（覆盖多样化编辑任务并首创多图编辑评测）、CPI-Practical-Bench（聚焦高频真实用户应用场景）、CPI-Intelligent-Bench（评测高要求推理型编辑能力）。

Result: 基于CPI-Bench对主流图像编辑模型的评测表明，该基准增强了模型间的性能区分度，并在排名分析中与Arena Image Edit Leaderboard达到最高一致性，反映出对人类主观评价的高度对齐能力。

Conclusion: CPI-Bench为图像编辑模型的通用能力、实际部署效果和高级推理编辑能力提供了全面可靠的量化评估，可作为真实用户体验的强代理指标，为未来模型优化提供有价值的指导。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of image editing models and their widespread application across various domains, there is an increasingly urgent need to deploy these model capabilities directly into real-world scenarios. However, existing benchmarks remain confined to simple single-image tasks, suffering from limited coverage dimensions and an inability to effectively differentiate performance among diverse models. Consequently, they fail to reliably evaluate model performance in complex multi-image editing, highly demanding reasoning instructions, and practical deployment settings. To address these limitations, we propose CPI-Bench, a Comprehensive, Practical andIntelligent benchmark for real-world image editing. CPI-Bench comprises three core subsets: CPI-General-Bench, which comprehensively covers diverse editing tasks and pioneers the inclusion of multi-image editing evaluation; CPI-Practical-Bench, which focuses on high-frequency real-user application scenarios; and CPI-Intelligent-Bench, which is dedicated to evaluating capabilities in highly demanding reasoning-based editing. Evaluation results of mainstream image editing models based on CPI-Bench demonstrate that CPI-Bench enhances performance differentiation among models. It provides a comprehensive and reliable quantification of gaps in general editing capabilities, practical deployment efficacy, and advanced reasoning-based editing, offering invaluable guidance for the future optimization of image editing models. Crucially, our ranking analysis reveals that CPI-Bench achieves the highest alignment with the Arena Image Edit Leaderboard, indicating it faithfully captures the preferences and perceptual judgments of human evaluators, serving as a robust proxy for real-world user experience.

</details>

### [68] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RubricForge通过反射式进化算法，从少量带有真实环境奖励标注的轨迹中诱导生成可读的评分规则文本，用于零奖励环境下的LLM智能体评估。与G-Eval等现有方法相比，虽在原始一致性上无显著优势，但在错误放行率（false-pass rate）上显著更低，诚实度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估语言模型智能体时，通常依赖另一个LLM作为自动评分器（judge），因为可执行环境奖励过于昂贵、缓慢或在部署时不可用。现有方法（如G-Eval手写评分规则或微调judge权重）倾向于给流畅但不成功的轨迹打分过高，缺乏可信度。因此需要一种基于真实结果而非流利度打分的方法。

Method: RubricForge的核心方法：(1) 利用少量带真实标签的轨迹（tau-bench 173条、WebShop 160条）；(2) 通过反射式进化（reflective evolution）迭代优化评分规则（rubric）文本，以最大化与真实环境奖励的一致性；(3) 冻结优化后的规则，在对未见轨迹评估时仅需一次模型调用，无需环境访问；(4) 生成的规则是人工可读文本，每个评判结果可以归因到具名标准。

Result: 使用同一个冻结的7B模型作为agent和judge：在tau-bench上，RubricForge的false-pass rate为0.115，显著低于G-Eval的0.173；在WebShop上Spearman相关系数从0.370提升至0.410。但与G-Eval的整体一致性差异不显著（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准略逊于通用judge（|err|差异-0.048，p=2e-4）。在tau-bench上捕获了三次过度放行且无反转。

Conclusion: 对于无奖励的评估器，false-pass rate（错误放行率）而非总体一致性才是部署相关的核心指标——放行一个失败的智能体意味着部署了有缺陷的系统，而误判失败仅需重试。RubricForge的优势在于忠实性（faithfulness）而非原始一致性，其可读规则文本提供了可归因性的额外价值。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [69] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（40层MoE，每层256个专家，top-8路由）进行了系统的层敏感度分析，发现MoE层的敏感性呈深度依赖：浅层和中层对专家掩码高度脆弱，而深层（30-39层）尤其是35-39层能容忍激进的低幅值专家掩码。晚期聚焦策略在保留更高质量输出的同时掩码了640-1145个专家；此外将top-k路由从8降到6可显著降低墙钟时间且不损失输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构已被广泛用于扩展LLM并保持计算效率，但各MoE层在压缩场景下的相对重要性尚未被充分刻画，缺乏系统性、深度感知的层敏感度理解。

Method: 在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上，对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型进行基于幅值的专家掩码层敏感度分析；采用多阶段评估（100、300、500 prompt规模）和不同层区间（early/mid/late/very-late）的掩码策略对比；同时测试top-k路由宽度从8降至6的效果。

Result: 浅层（0-9）和中层（10-29）对专家掩码高度脆弱；30%全层掩码仅保留150/300的Good+Similar输出，而晚期聚焦策略保留249-255/300同时掩码640-1145个专家；500-prompt验证集上，very-late策略（35-39层@50%）掩码640/10240个专家保留419/500输出；top-k从8降至6在100-prompt上无质量损失且大幅降低墙钟时间，但与激进专家掩码组合尚不协调。

Conclusion: MoE层敏感性呈强深度依赖，为深度感知的MoE专家掩码提供了实证基础，并为物理权重剪枝、基于激活的专家评分和训练后恢复指明了实际路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [70] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过电路分析，在46个涵盖语言、形式推理、社会推理和物理推理四个认知领域的任务中，发现大语言模型（LLM）发展出了与人类大脑相似的模块化架构：人类使用相同网络的任务在LLM中招募重叠的神经元，而使用不同网络的任务则招募不同的神经元，这表明模块化可能是智能系统的基本属性而不仅仅是生物大脑的进化偶然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类大脑展现出显著的功能特化，不同网络分别支持语言、形式推理、心智推理和物理推理。作者希望探究这种模块化组织究竟是一切智能系统的根本构建原则，还是仅存在于生物大脑的进化偶然事件。通过在LLM这一通过截然不同优化过程产生的智能系统中检验类似组织是否出现，可以回答这一根本性问题。

Method: 对LLM进行电路分析（circuit analyses），覆盖N=46个任务，横跨四个认知域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）。比较人类大脑网络与LLM神经元招募模式之间的对应关系，检验任务间神经元重叠程度是否与人类大脑中网络共享模式一致。

Result: LLM确实发展出了与人类大脑镜像的模块化架构：在人类中调用相同任务网络的任务，在LLM中也招募重叠的神经元；在人类中调用不同网络的任务，在LLM中招募不同的神经元。这种跨系统的一致性表明模块化结构在两种截然不同的智能系统中均自发涌现。

Conclusion: 模块化组织在生物大脑与人工神经网络中的趋同涌现，表明它可能是智能系统的根本属性，而非特定于生物进化的偶然设计。这一发现对理解智能的普适原理具有重要意义。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>


### [71] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析来自Chutes的一整年生产环境追踪数据，对真实世界LLM服务负载进行了全面的全球特征描述和纵向研究。与以往研究不同，该追踪数据覆盖了众多模型和用户的完整生产行为（包括热门和长尾模型），并从聚合、时间、模型级别和用户级别四个视角进行分析，揭示了通常在聚合视图中被隐藏的工作负载演变和用户-模型结构。论文将随发布完整的一年追踪数据，支持下游研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务负载研究在规模（观察时间短）和范围（对用户与模型交互方式有限可见性）上均受限，无法充分捕捉LLM服务负载随时间的演变以及用户-模型交互如何塑造生产流量。真实追踪数据对激励和基准测试服务系统至关重要，但缺乏覆盖大规模生产环境的长期追踪数据。

Method: 本文基于Chutes平台收集的一年期生产追踪数据进行研究，从四个分析视角展开：(1)聚合视角分析整体负载特征；(2)时间视角分析负载随时间的演变；(3)模型级别视角分析不同模型（包括长尾模型）的负载模式；(4)用户级别视角分析用户行为与交互结构。

Result: 研究揭示了在生产环境中被聚合视图通常隐藏的工作负载演变模式和用户-模型交互结构，覆盖范围包括热门模型和长尾模型，提供了对真实世界LLM服务负载更全面的理解。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一次跨越一年的生产级LLM服务追踪数据集，并从多维度进行了全面分析，将完整发布该追踪数据以支持未来研究，使下游研究能够基于真实生产行为而非采样或合成数据进行。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [72] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agentao是一个受治理的本地优先运行时系统，用于工具调用型LLM代理，通过分层架构将模型生成的行动提案与宿主授权的执行分离，使权限、状态、协议边界和执行追踪成为显式运行时抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理作为执行系统运行时存在过度特权操作、弱可审计性、提示注入、工具投毒和不受控制的副作用等风险，需要一种能增强代理可治理性和可检查性的运行时架构。

Method: 采用分层架构，包括宿主面对接口层、宿主契约、运行时核心、权限中介工具系统，以及支持内存、回放、插件、技能、子代理和协议集成的子系统，将模型生成的行动提案与宿主授权的执行分离。

Result: 演示了如何将权限、状态、协议边界和执行追踪构建为显式运行时抽象，使代理更可治理、可检查且适合宿主控制的本地环境，但系统不提供正式的安全保证。

Conclusion: Agentao通过显式运行时抽象实现了更可治理和可检查的LLM代理，代码已公开，但不承诺正式安全保证，而是作为构建可控代理的参考架构。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [73] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI评估应从追求超人类自主表现（以替代人类为目标）转向评估人机协作团队的表现，以引导AI发展为人类能力的真正补充，从而产生更好的社会结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估的主流范式聚焦于超人类自主性能，隐含地以替代人类为目标，这正在将AI发展引向错误方向。作者认为这种范式需要根本性转变。

Method: 本文是一篇立场论文（position paper），通过论证和逻辑推理提出：AI评估应转向人机协作（human-AI team）场景下的团队绩效评估。

Result: 提出将评估重心从自主超人类表现转向人机协作团队表现，以推动AI系统成为人类能力的真正补充。

Conclusion: 协作范式的转变将带来远优于当前过程的AI发展路径和社会成果。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [74] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中"高置信度错误"的不同成因：提出"稳定误校准"假设，即自信但错误的答案在小扰动下仍保持局部稳定，而非仅在脆弱推理下出错。作者结合两种诊断方法（输出级审计评分和隐藏状态敏感性探针），在多域二分类事实审计集上验证了该假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的高置信度错误通常被视为内部推理脆弱的证据。作者质疑这一默认假设，提出另一种可能：某些错误是"稳定误校准"（stable miscalibration），即错误答案在局部扰动下依然稳定。区分这两种情形对理解模型可靠性至关重要。

Method: （1）输出级审计评分：一种标签感知的审计得分，基于强制回答基线，按置信度变异和过度自信错误对领域排序。（2）内部敏感性探针：测量隐藏状态在扰动下的移动程度。在多个开源模型上，将自我批评提示（self-critique prompting）作为干预手段，比较输出层决策损失与内部隐藏状态敏感性的变化。

Result: 审计评分能够追踪自我批评提示降低决策损失的领域，但直接带标签的基线更能区分这一增益。在三个开源模型中，自我批评提示一致地降低了各层的隐藏状态敏感性，支持"提示诱导局部稳定化"而非单纯输出层面弃权模式。然而，审计定义的过度自信错误与自信正确的答案在局部敏感性上无显著差异，表明部分高置信度错误是稳定且误校准的。

Conclusion: 高置信度错误并非全部源于脆弱推理，部分可能是稳定的误校准。自我批评提示通过局部稳定化降低内部敏感性，但这并不等于改善校准。研究提示需要将"稳定性"与"校准性"作为两个独立的模型可靠性诊断维度分别考量。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference. We study a different possibility: stable miscalibration, where a confident wrong answer remains locally stable under small perturbations. We combine two diagnostics: a label-aware output-level audit score that ranks domains by confidence variation and overconfident mistakes under a forced-answer baseline, and an internal sensitivity probe that measures hidden-state movement. On a multi-domain binary factual audit set, this audit score tracks where abstention-aware self-critique reduces decision loss, although direct labeled baselines rank the same gain more strongly. Internally, self-critical prompting consistently reduces hidden-state sensitivity across layers in three open-weight models. This supports prompt-induced local stabilization rather than a purely output-level abstention pattern, but it does not imply calibration: audit-defined overconfident errors are not clearly more locally sensitive than confidently correct answers, so some high-confidence errors may be stable and miscalibrated rather than simply fragile.

</details>


### [75] [Measuring Cross-Task Behavioral Consistency in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13598)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Pranil Raichura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为一致性指标（BCM），通过从智能体执行轨迹的特征中训练模型预测任务成败并计算特征归因向量的两两相似度，来量化智能体在跨任务上的行为一致性。实验基于约9000条来自6种LM智能体的软件工程轨迹，发现跨任务一致性与同任务可复现性是相互独立的不同维度，且一致性无法被成功率化约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体评估几乎完全依赖结果指标（如成功率），只能反映"能否成功"，无法反映"行为是否稳定一致"。作者认为行为一致性是一个独立且可量化的性质，值得被系统性地度量。

Method: BCM首先训练一个模型，从智能体执行轨迹的行为特征出发预测任务是否成功；然后为每条轨迹导出逐特征的归因向量；最后在智能体系统内计算这些归因向量之间的平均两两相似度，作为一致性度量。

Result: 在约9000条来自六个LM智能体的软件工程任务轨迹上：（1）跨任务一致性与同任务内可复现性是独立轴，二者可能背离——部分系统有局部可复现性但全局碎片化；（2）一致性不能化约为成功率，成功率相近的系统一致性可差异显著；（3）在控制任务难度的同任务条件下，前沿模型与开源模型的一致性差距仍然存在。

Conclusion: BCM可作为过程层面的可靠性信号，与结果指标互补。同时作者明确指出了该指标适用的条件边界（何时有意义、何时可能产生误导），强调行为一致性应当成为智能体评估中独立的考量维度。

Abstract: Agent evaluation relies almost entirely on outcome metrics such as success rate, which capture whether an agent succeeds but not how consistently it behaves. We argue that behavioral consistency across tasks is a distinct and measurable property, and we introduce the Behavioral Consistency Metric (BCM) to quantify it. BCM trains a model to predict task success from behavioral features of agent execution traces, derives a per-trajectory feature-attribution vector, and measures the mean pairwise similarity of these vectors within an agent system. Across roughly 9,000 trajectories from six language model agents on software engineering tasks, our central finding is that cross-task and within-task consistency are distinct axes that can diverge: some systems are locally reproducible, behaving similarly on repeated attempts at one task, yet globally fragmented, with no stable strategy across different tasks, while others are consistent at both scales. Prior work measures only same-task reproducibility and so cannot observe this separation. We further find that consistency is not reducible to success rate, since systems with comparable success can differ sharply in consistency, and that the frontier-versus-open-source consistency gap persists under a within-task control that holds task difficulty constant. We position BCM as a process-level reliability signal that complements outcome metrics, and we are explicit about the conditions under which it is meaningful.

</details>


### [76] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地分析"误解"这一沟通问题，将其建模为一个分层过程（分歧产生→放大→被检测修复或持续不被注意），整合九个互不引用的研究领域，识别出11种精确故障模式，分布在由文献推导出的8个分析层上，并给出形式化模型、证据矩阵、编码手册及9个对话案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沟通正在从实时、面对面的互动转向AI中介的渠道，这种转变切断了沟通者依赖的修复资源，而新检测手段的建设速度跟不上需求，因此误解检测成为一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 作者整合了九个通常互不引用的研究领域的文献，识别出11种故障模式；根据每个模式在沟通过程中的具体作用点推导出8个分析层（非套用现有模型）；将信息与传播理论从信号传输扩展到意义重建，对8个层进行形式化建模；并提供逐来源证据矩阵、编码手册及9个对话案例以保证可审计性和可复现性。

Result: 确定了11种误解机制，其中8种主要产生分歧，2种主要放大已有分歧，1种决定分歧是否被检测与修复；每个机制都作用于沟通过程中的特定点而非任意位置，并据此构建了8个分析层及形式化模型。

Conclusion: 本文提出的误解分类法同时具备两个先验分类所不具备的特征：既将机制定位在沟通过程的具体节点上，又按功能对机制进行类型划分，为AI中介沟通环境下的误解检测提供了结构化的理论框架与工具。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases. No prior classification of misunderstanding both locates mechanisms at points in the process and types them by function.

</details>


### [77] [Active Perception for Embodied Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13605)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于主动感知的具身目标消歧框架，利用视觉语言模型根据累积的视觉证据和交互信息，在继续观察、请求澄清和完成目标选择之间做决策，将物理信息获取与用户意图澄清统一在同一消歧流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式消歧方法主要依赖向用户提问来获取额外信息，但具身环境中的目标歧义不仅源于用户意图，还可能因遮挡、受限视角、不可读文本等导致当前观察中缺乏与任务相关的物理证据。机器人需要主动改变其观察以缓解此类歧义，而这在现有方法中未被充分解决。

Method: 提出以主动观察为核心信息获取手段的具身目标消歧框架，使用视觉语言模型基于累积的视觉证据和交互信息进行决策，决定下一步是继续观察、请求澄清，还是完成目标选择。主动观察既能直接恢复缺失的判别性证据，也能揭示对象名称、标签和语义属性，从而在仍需澄清时改进用户交互质量。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，该框架能够在统一的具身消歧过程中有效融合物理信息获取与用户意图澄清。

Conclusion: 主动感知可作为具身目标消歧的核心策略，将物理观察与用户澄清有机整合，为解决复杂环境下的目标歧义问题提供了有效的统一框架。

Abstract: Natural language provides robots with a flexible task interface, but target ambiguity in embodied environments arises not only from user intent; it can also result from missing taskrelevant physical evidence in the current observation. Existing interactive disambiguation methods primarily obtain additional information by asking the user, whereas occlusion, restricted viewpoints, unreadable text, and unobserved targets require the robot to actively change its observation. We propose an active-perception framework for embodied target disambiguation that uses active observation as the backbone for information acquisition and uses a vision-language model to decide, on the basis of accumulated visual evidence and interaction information, whether to continue observing, request clarification, or complete target selection. Active observation can both directly recover missing discriminative evidence and reveal object names, labels, and semantic attributes, thereby improving user clarification when it remains necessary. Real-robot experiments show that the framework combines physical information acquisition and userintent clarification within a unified embodied disambiguation process.

</details>


### [78] [MobileMem: Learning from a Year of Mobile Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13606)
*Xinle Deng,Yida Xue,Xiangyuan Ru,Haoming Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Mengru Wang,Yijun Chen,Buqiang Xu,Chen Jiang,Yuchen Eleanor Jiang,Lizhong Wang,Jianfeng Wang,Li Zeng,Haofen Wang,Guilin Qi,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileMem是一个面向设备端长期记忆的基准测试与框架，基于年尺度移动体验数据，采用知识驱动的合成流水线生成连贯且时序一致的长期轨迹，涵盖文本与多模态设置，支持多跳推理、时序推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断，推动AI代理从信息检索走向经验智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代AI代理正从回答孤立问题转向能够理解、记忆并从用户经验中持续学习的持久个人助手。这类助手需要长期记忆来积累和利用用户特定经验，但现有基准无法满足真实移动场景的需求——这些场景中的经验是异构、多模态、动态演变且高度个性化的。

Method: MobileMem提出一个基准测试与框架，包含：1）基于年尺度移动体验数据集的构建；2）知识驱动的合成流水线，用于生成连贯且时序一致的长期轨迹；3）文本与多模态互补设置，覆盖多跳推理、时序推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断四类任务。

Result: MobileMem使代理能够记住过去、理解现在并适应未来，通过建模经验而非孤立事实，将记忆从信息检索层面提升到经验智能层面，支持持续的个人学习。

Conclusion: MobileMem通过建模经验而非孤立事实，将长期记忆从信息检索推进到经验智能，为设备端持续个人学习提供了研究与评估的基础设施。

Abstract: The next generation of AI agents is increasingly moving beyond systems that answer isolated questions toward persistent personal assistants that can understand, remember, and continuously learn from users' experiences. Such assistants require long-term memory to accumulate and leverage user-specific experiences over time, yet existing benchmarks remain inadequate for realistic mobile settings, where experiences are heterogeneous, multimodal, evolving, and deeply personal. We introduce MobileMem, a benchmark and framework for studying on-device long-term memory, grounded in a year-scale collection of mobile experiences. MobileMem employs a knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline to construct coherent and temporally consistent long-horizon trajectories from user-app sessions. It provides complementary text and multimodal settings covering multi-hop and temporal reasoning, knowledge updating, and implicit preference inference. Specifically, MobileMem enables agents to remember the past, understand the present, and adapt to the future. By modeling experiences rather than isolated facts, MobileMem moves memory beyond information retrieval toward experiential intelligence for continuous personal learning.

</details>


### [79] [No Universal Signal Predicts Sample-Level LLM Regression under Version Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13607)
*Jia Sheng,Yiwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在推理时预测前沿大语言模型更新导致的样本级性能回退（regression），通过统一添加值测试比较单模型信号（置信度、logit边际、注意力熵）与跨版本信号（输出KL散度、似然漂移、token级KL、表征漂移）。在6个基准、3个任务族、6个模型更新对上的实验表明：信号有效性依赖任务类型，没有信号在所有更新中普遍最优，部分跨版本信号在置信度失效时仍保持信息量，支持将高风险样本回退到旧模型的选择性回退方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM频繁更新且在总体上优于前代，但总体进步无法反映单样本层面的回退：旧模型回答正确而新模型回答错误的情况仍然存在。此前研究缺乏对各类回归预测信号的系统比较，且未隔离各信号相对于简单置信度基线的增量价值，因此需要统一框架来评估和选择有效的回归预测信号。

Method: 作者提出统一的添加值（added-value）测试来隔离每个信号相对于置信度基线的独立增益。比较两类信号：单模型信号（置信度、logit边际、注意力熵）和跨版本信号（输出KL散度、似然漂移、token级KL、表征漂移）。实验覆盖多选题问答、数学推理、代码生成三个任务族，共6个基准和6个模型更新对，并在无标签设置下评估信号的信息量。

Result: 主要发现：（1）信号效果依赖任务：置信度在MCQ和较简单数学上最强，似然/KL信号在较难数学和代码上增益最频繁；（2）没有任何信号在所有模型更新中普遍最优；（3）部分跨版本信号在置信度失效时仍保持信息性（包括无标签场景），支持构建概念验证的选择性回退机制，将高风险样本路由回旧模型。

Conclusion: 任务层面的信号有效性模式可以为实践者提供指导，帮助其为特定模型更新选择合适的回归预测信号。跨版本信号在无标签场景下的稳健性为安全部署新模型版本提供了实用路径，可通过选择性回退减少更新带来的性能退化。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are updated frequently and typically outperform their predecessors in aggregate. But aggregate gains say little about individual samples: an update can still cause sample-level regression, where a response correct under the old model becomes incorrect under the new one. This paper studies how to predict such regressions from signals available at inference time. We compare single-model signals (confidence, logit margin, attention entropy) against cross-version signals (output KL divergence, likelihood drift, token-level KL, representation drift) under a unified added-value test that isolates each signal's gain over a confidence baseline. Across six benchmarks in three task families (multiple-choice question answering, or MCQ; math reasoning; code generation) and six model update pairs, we find that (1) signal effectiveness is task-dependent: confidence is strongest on MCQ and simpler math, while likelihood/KL signals give the most frequent gains on harder math and code; (2) no signal is universally best across model updates either; and (3) some cross-version signals stay informative even when confidence fails, including without labels, which supports a proof-of-concept selective fallback that routes high-risk samples back to the old model. Practitioners can use these task-level patterns to choose which regression signal to trust for a given update. Code is available at https://github.com/jiashengsally/llm-regression-signals.

</details>


### [80] [Evaluating Agentic Learning Harness Capabilities Without Labels via the Scaling Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13608)
*Aryan Luthra,Kshitij Jain,Siddharth Arya,Bobby Filar,Anna Bertiger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需标注基准的评估框架，用于衡量网络安全领域智能体持续学习系统（LLM+检索/记忆）的提升效果。通过强教师模型对弱学生模型提供稀疏修正，以学生向教师收敛的速度作为得分，验证了教师相对提升可作为无标签场景下真实提升的代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全场景中，标注基准稀缺、过时且不具备代表性，导致实践者无法判断持续学习系统是否有效或如何选择更优方案。传统的LLM-as-a-judge因评价者能力不弱于被评对象而缺乏信号，蒸馏方法在稀疏、零星且有偏的标签上也不可靠，因此需要一种不依赖标注基准的端到端评估方法。

Method: 提出基于缩放假说的评估框架：以更强教师模型向带持续学习系统的小型学生模型提供稀疏采样修正，通过衡量学生随时间向教师收敛的程度来评分harness。在安全任务、模型家族和harness设计等多个维度上，验证教师相对提升与留出黄金标准提升之间的相关性。

Result: 实验表明：（1）相对于教师的提升与相对于保留黄金标准的提升相关联，验证了教师相对提升可作为无标签场景下真实harness提升的代理；（2）能力相近模型之间的LLM-as-a-judge无法产生可用信号。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，当人类提供相同的稀疏、高精度修正时，教师规模的模型可以通过相同的harness得到提升，这为无标签网络安全环境下的持续学习系统评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Agentic "Continual Learning Harnesses", systems that pair an LLM with retrieval or memory to improve from feedback without retraining, have shown growing value in cybersecurity. But their value is conventionally measured by gains against labeled benchmarks, an approach that often fails in operational security settings. Benchmark labels are scarce, stale, and unrepresentative, so a practitioner often cannot tell whether a given harness helps at all or which of two is better for their task. Traditional LLM-as-a-judge offers little signal because it is no stronger than the agent it evaluates, and distillation is unreliable on scarce, sporadic, and biased labels.
  We propose a framework for evaluating learning harnesses end-to-end without a labeled benchmark, grounded in the scaling hypothesis. A stronger teacher model provides sparsely sampled corrections to a smaller student with a continual learning harness. We score a harness by how much its student converges toward the teacher over time. Across security tasks, model families, and harness designs, we show that improvement relative to the teacher correlates with improvement relative to a held-out gold standard, validating teacher-relative lift as a proxy for true harness uplift when labels are absent. We further show that LLM-as-a-judge between similarly powered models yields no usable signal. These results suggest that a teacher-sized model can be improved through the same harness when humans provide the same kind of sparse, high-precision corrections.

</details>


### [81] [SemPlan: Benchmarking Structured Semantic Planning for LLM-Based Queries over Enterprise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13612)
*Bruno Santos Teixeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemPlan Benchmark，一个包含1,800个中英葡双语的确定性合成基准，系统比较了四种自然语言到企业数据接口的架构（直接SQL生成、有界工具Agent、结构化语义请求+确定性规划执行、澄清/有状态语义规划变体）。结果显示各架构绝对正确率都较低（22%-26%），A3正确率最高但A1策略合规性最优，A4成本最低，整体呈现权衡而非统一排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据的自然语言接口需要将模糊的用户请求转化为受治理的可执行行为，同时控制无效查询、策略失败、成本和不确定性。然而该架构设计空间缺乏系统性评估，现有研究未能在受控双语环境下比较不同架构的性能权衡。

Method: 构建确定性合成双语基准（英文+巴西葡萄牙语），含1,800个案例（1,200个冻结核�学评估子集）。在相同模型配置下比较四种架构：A1（直接SQL生成）、A2（有界工具Agent基线）、A3（结构化语义请求生成+确定性规划执行）、A4（澄清/有状态语义规划变体）。基于4,800条主记录及150案例稳定性子集进行多维度评估。

Result: 绝对答案正确率普遍偏低：A1为22.25%、A2为22.58%、A3为25.67%、A4为24.25%。A3正确率最高且在预设配对分析中显著优于其他三者；A1策略合规率最高且不安全/无效率最低；A4平均API成本最低且错误拒绝率最低。稳定性子集上可重复性为92%-98.67%。

Conclusion: 结果支持权衡解释而非统一排名：额外的结构约束改变了失败模式和效率，但并未单调提升正确率，也无法解决歧义和多轮状态一致性问题。架构选择需根据具体需求（正确性、合规性、成本）权衡决定。

Abstract: Natural-language interfaces to enterprise data must translate underspecified requests into governed, executable behavior while controlling invalid queries, policy failures, cost, and nondeterminism. SemPlan Benchmark evaluates this architectural design space with a deterministic synthetic bilingual benchmark containing 1,800 cases in English and Brazilian Portuguese; 1,200 cases form the frozen scientific evaluation subset. Four architectures are compared under the same model configuration: direct SQL generation (A1), a bounded tool-agent baseline (A2), structured semantic-request generation followed by deterministic planning and execution (A3), and a clarification/stateful semantic-plan variant (A4). Across 4,800 primary records, answer correctness was low in absolute terms: 22.25% for A1, 22.58% for A2, 25.67% for A3, and 24.25% for A4. A3 had the highest observed correctness and significantly exceeded A1, A2, and A4 in the pre-specified paired correctness analysis, while A1 retained the highest policy-correct rate and the lowest unsafe-or-invalid rate. A4 had the lowest mean API cost and lowest false-refusal rate. On a preselected 150-case stability subset, answer-correct repeatability ranged from 92.00% to 98.67%. The results support a trade-off interpretation rather than a universal ranking: additional structural constraints changed failure modes and efficiency, but did not monotonically improve correctness or solve ambiguity and multi-turn state consistency.

</details>


### [82] [How Compliant is Sepsis Treatment? An Expert-Guided Neuro-symbolic Pipeline for Generating Clinical Compliance Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13617)
*Himanshu Tripathi,Kaushik Roy,Subash Neupane,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种专家引导的神经符号管道，将大语言模型严格限制为语义归一化任务（将药物和微生物学字符串映射到固定临床词汇表），随后由Sugeno模糊推理系统对归一化事件进行推理，编码八条脓毒症救治bundle规则，用[0,1]的等级评分替代二元判定。在2,438个MIMIC-IV v3.1脓毒症病例上应用，发现抗生素时机是最关键的失败环节（均值0.24，仅13%在一小时内）、Hour-1表现欠佳（均值36.7%）、高乳酸检测51%的断崖式下降，以及不同依从组间ICU住院天数存在差异（3.8天vs 5.1天）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证临床护理是否符合循证方案本质上是神经符号问题，但在安全关键场景中，纯神经方法或纯符号方法单独使用均会失败，需要将两者的优势融合以可靠地评估临床依从性。

Method: 采用专家引导的流水线：(1) 将大语言模型严格约束为语义归一化器，把杂乱药物与微生物字符串映射到固定临床词汇表；(2) 以Sugeno模糊推理系统对归一化事件进行推理，编码八条重症脓毒症救治bundle规则；(3) 用连续[0,1]的模糊评分替代二元判定，实现对依从性的分级评估。

Result: 在2,438例MIMIC-IV v3.1脓毒症病例中，抗生素时机均值仅0.24且仅13%在一小时内达标，是最关键的失败环节；Hour-1整体表现均值为36.7%；高乳酸检测存在51%的落差；依从性高低组间ICU住院时长分别为3.8天与5.1天。

Conclusion: 该神经符号管道能够有效识别脓毒症护理过程中最关键的依从性缺口，量化各环节的达标程度，并揭示依从性与临床结局（如ICU住院时长）之间的关联，为改进循证护理提供了可操作的诊断证据。

Abstract: Verifying whether clinical care follows evidence-based protocols is a natural neuro-symbolic problem, yet the safety-critical setting defeats either paradigm alone. We present an expert-guided pipeline that constrains a large language model strictly to semantic normalization, mapping messy drug and microbiology strings onto a fixed clinical vocabulary, while a Sugeno fuzzy inference system reasons over the normalized events. The fuzzy layer encodes eight Surviving Sepsis Campaign bundle rules and replaces binary judgments with graded scores in [0,1]. Applied to 2,438 MIMIC-IV v3.1 sepsis episodes, it surfaces antibiotic timing as the most critical breakdown (mean 0.24, 13% within one hour), Hour-1 underperformance (mean 36.7%), a 51% elevated-lactate drop-off, and descriptive differences in ICU stay across compliance groups (3.8 versus 5.1 days).

</details>


### [83] [Algorithm Design and Physician Liability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13618)
*Shujie Luan,Shubhranshu Singh,Tinglong Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究美国新出台的问责规则如何影响AI医疗算法的设计（开发者决策）与使用（医生决策）。研究发现，该责任规则可能导致AI的差异化使用：医生在整体上减少AI使用，且在中间责任区间内对弱势群体患者更少依赖AI；随着责任增加，医生对弱势群体的AI使用先降后升（非单调效应）。强制算法跨群体等精度可能反而伤害所有患者群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一临床算法在不同患者群体间可能提供不等的诊断准确率，随着AI在临床决策中的普及，这种算法差异引发的公平性担忧日益加剧。美国引入了一项责任规则，使医疗提供者因依赖有差异的算法而导致的错误临床决策承担责任，因此需要系统研究该责任规则对AI开发与使用行为的重塑作用。

Method: 构建了一个两阶段博弈模型：AI企业为两个患者群体设计算法，提高弱势群体准确率的成本更高；医生作为最终负责的决策者，在权衡临床不确定性降低与预期责任暴露后决定是否使用AI。模型分析包括责任规则下医生的AI使用决策、企业的投资分配决策以及强制等精度政策的比较分析。

Result: 责任规则可诱导差异化AI使用：医生可能整体减少AI使用，在中间责任范围内对弱势群体患者的AI依赖更少；AI使用对责任的响应是非单调的——先随责任增加下降，后因企业重新配置投资以缩小差距或转向等精度设计而回升。强制等精度设计会扭曲企业投资激励与医生的均衡使用决策，反而对两类群体都有害。

Conclusion: 责任规则对算法医疗中的公平性具有双向作用：适度的责任压力可促使企业减少算法差异，但过强或一刀切的等精度强制要求可能带来适得其反的结果。政策制定者应认识到责任规则与算法公平性之间的复杂、非单调互动，避免简单化的强制公平措施。
